```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/artifacts/arr_checklist_raw.md",
  "content": "# ARR Reproducibility Checklist — Raw Materials\n\n## 1. Training Hardware & Runtime\n\n**Server**: westd-11235 (2× NVIDIA GPU, local)\n**GPU allocation**: cuda:0 + cuda:1\n\n### Per-condition training time (Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct, 3 epochs, seed=42)\n\n| Condition | Wall Clock (s) | GPU Hours | Notes |\n|-----------|---------------|-----------|-------|\n| no_trace | ~1,275 | 0.35 | shortest (no trace tokens) |\n| exception_only | 4,541–7,507 | 1.3–2.1 | varies by seed |\n| ae_only | ~6,398 | ~1.8 | similar to exception_only |\n| loop_only | 7,426 | 2.06 | |\n| vota_no_ae | 6,398 | 1.78 | |\n| VOTA (full) | 8,443 | 2.35 | longest (most trace tokens) |\n| full_trace | 7,852 | 2.18 | |\n| label_only (full_trace + loss mask) | ~7,800 | ~2.2 | similar to full_trace |\n\n### DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct (3 epochs, seed=42)\n\n| Condition | Wall Clock (s) | GPU Hours |\n|-----------|---------------|-----------|\n| no_trace | ~1,200 | ~0.33 |\n| exception_only | 6,246–6,384 | 1.7–1.8 |\n| VOTA | 8,840 | 2.46 |\n| full_trace (v3) | 8,891 | ~2.5 |\n\n### DebugBench (Qwen, 3 epochs, seed=42)\n\n| Condition | Wall Clock (s) | GPU Hours |\n|-----------|---------------|-----------|\n| no_trace | ~800 | ~0.22 |\n| exception_only | 837 | 0.23 |\n| VOTA | ~3,200 | ~0.9 |\n\n**Total GPU compute**: ~50 GPU-hours across all experiments (including failed/killed runs and seed variations).\n\n## 2. LoRA Hyperparameters\n\n| Parameter | Value |\n|-----------|-------|\n| LoRA rank (r) | 16 |\n| LoRA alpha | 32 |\n| LoRA dropout | 0.05 |\n| Target modules | q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, o_proj, gate_proj, up_proj, down_proj |\n| Learning rate | 2e-4 |\n| LR scheduler | cosine |\n| Warmup ratio | 0.03 |\n| Epochs | 3 |\n| Per-device batch size | 4 |\n| Gradient accumulation | 1 |\n| Max sequence length | 2,048 tokens |\n| Gradient checkpointing | Yes (for VOTA/exception_only) |\n| Optimizer | AdamW (8-bit via bitsandbytes) |\n| Weight decay | 0.01 |\n| bf16 | Yes |\n\n*Note: Verify exact values against `train/train_sft.py` defaults and command-line overrides.*\n\n## 3. Dataset Split Details\n\n### Main dataset (HumanEval + MBPP-sanitized)\n- **Problems**: 591 unique (164 HumanEval + 427 MBPP-sanitized)\n- **Candidate solutions**: Generated by Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct (10 per problem)\n- **Total samples**: 5,910 (591 × 10)\n- **Train/test split**: 80/20 → 4,720 train / 1,190 test\n- **Class distribution**: ~71.51% pass / ~28.49% fail (majority baseline = 71.51%)\n- **Deduplication**: Applied to training set (exact-match on normalized code)\n\n### DebugBench dataset\n- **Source**: DebugBench (Tian et al., 2024) — synthetically injected bugs in LeetCode solutions\n- **Bug types**: logic errors, reference errors, syntax errors\n- **Train/test split**: 1,622 train / 426 test\n- **Class distribution**: ~51.41% pass / ~48.59% fail (near balanced)\n\n## 4. Random Seed Strategy\n\n- **Primary seed**: 42 (all conditions)\n- **Robustness seeds**: 123, 456 (selected conditions)\n- **Seed-tested conditions**:\n  - exception_only (Qwen): seeds 42/123/456 → 99.83% ± 0.00 (perfectly robust, 2/1190 identical errors across all seeds)\n  - exception_only (DeepSeek): seeds 42/123/456 → 99.33%/91.09%/99.33% (seed 123 outlier, LoRA initialization sensitivity)\n  - VOTA (Qwen): seeds 42/456 → 99.41%/99.08%\n  - ae_only (Qwen): seed 42 → 99.92%; seeds 123/456 in progress\n  - label_only: seed 42 only (seeds 123/456 planned)\n- **Other conditions**: single seed (42)\n\n## 5. Evaluation Metrics Definitions\n\n| Metric | Definition |\n|--------|-----------|\n| **Accuracy** | Fraction of correctly classified samples (pass/fail) |\n| **Pass/Fail Precision** | TP/(TP+FP) for each class |\n| **Pass/Fail Recall** | TP/(TP+FN) for each class |\n| **Pass/Fail F1** | Harmonic mean of precision and recall per class |\n| **95% CI** | Wilson score interval for accuracy |\n| **ECE** | Expected Calibration Error — weighted average of |accuracy − confidence| across 10 equal-width bins |\n| **MCE** | Maximum Calibration Error — max |accuracy − confidence| across bins |\n| **Brier Score** | Mean squared error between predicted probability and true label |\n| **McNemar's Test** | Paired test for significance of accuracy difference between two models on same test set (with continuity correction) |\n| **Paired Bootstrap** | 10,000 bootstrap resamples, percentile CI, paired on sample IDs |\n\n## 6. Code & Data Availability Statement\n\n- **Code**: Training, evaluation, and data processing scripts available at [repository URL TBD]\n- **Models**: LoRA adapters for all conditions available at [HuggingFace URL TBD]\n- **Data**: Execution traces generated from HumanEval and MBPP-sanitized using Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct\n  - Raw trace collection via Python `sys.settrace` instrumentation\n  - VOTA compression via deterministic algorithm (no LLM in loop)\n  - DebugBench traces from DebugBench dataset's provided solutions\n- **Base models**: Publicly available\n  - Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct (HuggingFace)\n  - deepseek-ai/deepseek-coder-6.7b-instruct (HuggingFace)\n- **Compute**: All experiments reproducible on a single machine with 2× consumer/prosumer GPUs (~48GB total VRAM)\n- **Licensing**: [TBD — check HumanEval, MBPP, DebugBench licenses]\n"
}

        "content": "# ARR Reproducibility Checklist — Raw Materials\n\n## 1. Training Hardware & Runtime\n\n**Server**: westd-11235 (2× NVIDIA GPU, local)\n**GPU allocation**: cuda:0 + cuda:1\n\n### Per-condition training time (Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct, 3 epochs, seed=42)\n\n| Condition | Wall Clock (s) | GPU Hours | Notes |\n|-----------|---------------|-----------|-------|\n| no_trace | ~1,275 | 0.35 | shortest (no trace tokens) |\n| exception_only | 4,541–7,507 | 1.3–2.1 | varies by seed |\n| ae_only | ~6,398 | ~1.8 | similar to exception_only |\n| loop_only | 7,426 | 2.06 | |\n| vota_no_ae | 6,398 | 1.78 | |\n| VOTA (full) | 8,443 | 2.35 | longest (most trace tokens) |\n| full_trace | 7,852 | 2.18 | |\n| label_only (full_trace + loss mask) | ~7,800 | ~2.2 | similar to full_trace |\n\n### DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct (3 epochs, seed=42)\n\n| Condition | Wall Clock (s) | GPU Hours |\n|-----------|---------------|-----------|\n| no_trace | ~1,200 | ~0.33 |\n| exception_only | 6,246–6,384 | 1.7–1.8 |\n| VOTA | 8,840 | 2.46 |\n| full_trace (v3) | 8,891 | ~2.5 |\n\n### DebugBench (Qwen, 3 epochs, seed=42)\n\n| Condition | Wall Clock (s) | GPU Hours |\n|-----------|---------------|-----------|\n| no_trace | ~800 | ~0.22 |\n| exception_only | 837 | 0.23 |\n| VOTA | ~3,200 | ~0.9 |\n\n**Total GPU compute**: ~50 GPU-hours across all experiments (including failed/killed runs and seed variations).\n\n## 2. LoRA Hyperparameters\n\n| Parameter | Value |\n|-----------|-------|\n| LoRA rank (r) | 16 |\n| LoRA alpha | 32 |\n| LoRA dropout | 0.05 |\n| Target modules | q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, o_proj, gate_proj, up_proj, down_proj |\n| Learning rate | 2e-4 |\n| LR scheduler | cosine |\n| Warmup ratio | 0.03 |\n| Epochs | 3 |\n| Per-device batch size | 4 |\n| Gradient accumulation | 1 |\n| Max sequence length | 2,048 tokens |\n| Gradient checkpointing | Yes (for VOTA/exception_only) |\n| Optimizer | AdamW (8-bit via bitsandbytes) |\n| Weight decay | 0.01 |\n| bf16 | Yes |\n\n*Note: Verify exact values against `train/train_sft.py` defaults and command-line overrides.*\n\n## 3. Dataset Split Details\n\n### Main dataset (HumanEval + MBPP-sanitized)\n- **Problems**: 591 unique (164 HumanEval + 427 MBPP-sanitized)\n- **Candidate solutions**: Generated by Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct (10 per problem)\n- **Total samples**: 5,910 (591 × 10)\n- **Train/test split**: 80/20 → 4,720 train / 1,190 test\n- **Class distribution**: ~71.51% pass / ~28.49% fail (majority baseline = 71.51%)\n- **Deduplication**: Applied to training set (exact-match on normalized code)\n\n### DebugBench dataset\n- **Source**: DebugBench (Tian et al., 2024) — synthetically injected bugs in LeetCode solutions\n- **Bug types**: logic errors, reference errors, syntax errors\n- **Train/test split**: 1,622 train / 426 test\n- **Class distribution**: ~51.41% pass / ~48.59% fail (near balanced)\n\n## 4. Random Seed Strategy\n\n- **Primary seed**: 42 (all conditions)\n- **Robustness seeds**: 123, 456 (selected conditions)\n- **Seed-tested conditions**:\n  - exception_only (Qwen): seeds 42/123/456 → 99.83% ± 0.00 (perfectly robust, 2/1190 identical errors across all seeds)\n  - exception_only (DeepSeek): seeds 42/123/456 → 99.33%/91.09%/99.33% (seed 123 outlier, LoRA initialization sensitivity)\n  - VOTA (Qwen): seeds 42/456 → 99.41%/99.08%\n  - ae_only (Qwen): seed 42 → 99.92%; seeds 123/456 in progress\n  - label_only: seed 42 only (seeds 123/456 planned)\n- **Other conditions**: single seed (42)\n\n## 5. Evaluation Metrics Definitions\n\n| Metric | Definition |\n|--------|-----------|\n| **Accuracy** | Fraction of correctly classified samples (pass/fail) |\n| **Pass/Fail Precision** | TP/(TP+FP) for each class |\n| **Pass/Fail Recall** | TP/(TP+FN) for each class |\n| **Pass/Fail F1** | Harmonic mean of precision and recall per class |\n| **95% CI** | Wilson score interval for accuracy |\n| **ECE** | Expected Calibration Error — weighted average of |accuracy − confidence| across 10 equal-width bins |\n| **MCE** | Maximum Calibration Error — max |accuracy − confidence| across bins |\n| **Brier Score** | Mean squared error between predicted probability and true label |\n| **McNemar's Test** | Paired test for significance of accuracy difference between two models on same test set (with continuity correction) |\n| **Paired Bootstrap** | 10,000 bootstrap resamples, percentile CI, paired on sample IDs |\n\n## 6. Code & Data Availability Statement\n\n- **Code**: Training, evaluation, and data processing scripts available at [repository URL TBD]\n- **Models**: LoRA adapters for all conditions available at [HuggingFace URL TBD]\n- **Data**: Execution traces generated from HumanEval and MBPP-sanitized using Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct\n  - Raw trace collection via Python `sys.settrace` instrumentation\n  - VOTA compression via deterministic algorithm (no LLM in loop)\n  - DebugBench traces from DebugBench dataset's provided solutions\n- **Base models**: Publicly available\n  - Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct (HuggingFace)\n  - deepseek-ai/deepseek-coder-6.7b-instruct (HuggingFace)\n- **Compute**: All experiments reproducible on a single machine with 2× consumer/prosumer GPUs (~48GB total VRAM)\n- **Licensing**: [TBD — check HumanEval, MBPP, DebugBench licenses]\n",

        "content": "{\n  \"raw\": \"结果已生成并保存。确认 exception_only 是 seed42 版本 (99.33%)，VOTA 是 max_length=16384 版本 (84.45%)。\\n\\n## DeepSeek Paired Comparisons (n=1190, seed42)\\n\\n| Pair (A vs B) | Acc_A | Acc_B | Δ(A-B) | 95% CI | McNemar p | sig |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| VOTA vs exception_only | 84.45% | 99.33% | -14.87% | [-17.06, -12.77] | 6.86e-45 | *** |\\n| VOTA vs full_trace | 84.45% | 79.16% | +5.29% | [+2.35, +8.15] | 4.03e-04 | *** |\\n| VOTA vs no_trace | 84.45% | 73.36% | +11.09% | [+8.07, +14.20] | 2.50e-12 | *** |\\n| exception_only vs full_trace | 99.33% | 79.16% | +20.17% | [+17.82, +22.61] | 8.85e-64 | *** |\\n| exception_only vs no_trace | 99.33% | 73.36% | +25.97% | [+23.36, +28.66] | 8.49e-83 | *** |\\n| full_trace vs no_trace | 79.16% | 73.36% | +5.80% | [+3.61, +7.98] | 3.67e-07 | *** |\\n\\n所有 6 对比较均高度显著 (p < 0.001)。文件已保存至：\\n- `/root/trace-verdict-study/analysis/deepseek_paired_comparisons.json`\\n- `/root/trace-verdict-study/analysis/deepseek_paired_comparisons.txt`\"\n}",

```json
{
  "parentUuid": "a4df61af-6e8d-482b-b148-45477e369fe0",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "file",
    "filename": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/discussion.tex",
    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/discussion.tex",
        "content": "\\section{Discussion}\n\nVerdict prediction is fundamentally a fault detection task: the model must determine whether execution deviated from expected behavior.\nException events are the most explicit signal of such deviation---structured diagnostic moments that encode the nature and location of a fault.\nUnlike loop compressions or assertion-point values, which require the model to integrate information across multiple trace elements, exception events provide a self-contained verdict cue.\nThis explains why \\texttt{exception\\_only} matches or exceeds VOTA: adding non-exception trace components dilutes the model's attention on the diagnostic signal rather than providing complementary information.\n\nWe acknowledge that exception-based verdict prediction is, at its core, fault indicator presence detection: the model learns to map the presence or absence of a structured error message to a binary label.\nThe Tier~1 accuracy of ${\\geq}$99.8\\% is therefore unsurprising in isolation.\nThe contribution lies in two less obvious results: (a)~adding more trace information actively \\emph{hurts} performance (VOTA $<$ \\texttt{exception\\_only}, Table~\\ref{tab:ablation}), quantifying an information density dilution effect that has not been previously measured; and (b)~this dilution is confounded by tokenizer-driven trace truncation on DeepSeek (\\S\\ref{sec:cross_model}), which causes the majority of VOTA training samples to exceed the context window, truncating diagnostic trace content while preserving the verdict label.\nA regex baseline analysis sharpens this point: the strongest legitimate regex (exception type pattern-matching) achieves 93.61\\%, while a format-marker heuristic reaches 99.33\\% by exploiting a data formatting artifact rather than exception semantics (Appendix~\\ref{sec:regex_baselines}).\nThe 6.22pp gap between regex and SFT concentrates on boundary samples where exceptions are raised but caught by \\texttt{try/except}---SFT correctly classifies 6/8, confirming that the model learns to distinguish caught from uncaught exceptions rather than merely detecting exception presence.\n\nTraining dynamics further illustrate the complexity beneath surface-level metrics: on DeepSeek, two seeds converge to nearly identical final training losses (0.122 vs.\\ 0.123) yet differ by 8.24pp in accuracy, demonstrating that token-level loss can mask fundamental differences in sample-level classification ability (Appendix~\\ref{sec:training_dynamics}).\nThe \\texttt{exception\\_only} 0.50pp cross-model gap---unaffected by truncation since inputs average ${\\sim}$200 tokens---provides a clean measure of model capability differences, suggesting a design principle: trace representations should match the model capability floor, not the ceiling (Appendix~\\ref{sec:robustness_analysis}).\nAdditional analysis of irreducible errors is in Appendix~\\ref{sec:irreducible}.\n",
        "numLines": 17,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 17
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../docs/paper/discussion.tex"
  },

    "newString": "| LoRA rank (r) | 16 |\n| LoRA alpha | 32 |\n| LoRA dropout | 0.05 |\n| Target modules | q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, o_proj, gate_proj, up_proj, down_proj |\n| Learning rate | 2e-4 |\n| LR scheduler | linear |\n| Warmup ratio | 0.1 |\n| Epochs | 3 |\n| Per-device batch size | 4 |\n| Gradient accumulation | 8 (effective batch size = 32) |\n| Max sequence length | 2,048 tokens |\n| Gradient checkpointing | Yes (for VOTA/exception_only) |\n| Optimizer | AdamW (fused, via torch) |\n| Weight decay | 0.01 |\n| bf16 | Yes |",
    "originalFile": "# ARR Reproducibility Checklist — Raw Materials\n\n## 1. Training Hardware & Runtime\n\n**Server**: westd-11235 (2× NVIDIA GPU, local)\n**GPU allocation**: cuda:0 + cuda:1\n\n### Per-condition training time (Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct, 3 epochs, seed=42)\n\n| Condition | Wall Clock (s) | GPU Hours | Notes |\n|-----------|---------------|-----------|-------|\n| no_trace | ~1,275 | 0.35 | shortest (no trace tokens) |\n| exception_only | 4,541–7,507 | 1.3–2.1 | varies by seed |\n| ae_only | ~6,398 | ~1.8 | similar to exception_only |\n| loop_only | 7,426 | 2.06 | |\n| vota_no_ae | 6,398 | 1.78 | |\n| VOTA (full) | 8,443 | 2.35 | longest (most trace tokens) |\n| full_trace | 7,852 | 2.18 | |\n| label_only (full_trace + loss mask) | ~7,800 | ~2.2 | similar to full_trace |\n\n### DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct (3 epochs, seed=42)\n\n| Condition | Wall Clock (s) | GPU Hours |\n|-----------|---------------|-----------|\n| no_trace | ~1,200 | ~0.33 |\n| exception_only | 6,246–6,384 | 1.7–1.8 |\n| VOTA | 8,840 | 2.46 |\n| full_trace (v3) | 8,891 | ~2.5 |\n\n### DebugBench (Qwen, 3 epochs, seed=42)\n\n| Condition | Wall Clock (s) | GPU Hours |\n|-----------|---------------|-----------|\n| no_trace | ~800 | ~0.22 |\n| exception_only | 837 | 0.23 |\n| VOTA | ~3,200 | ~0.9 |\n\n**Total GPU compute**: ~50 GPU-hours across all experiments (including failed/killed runs and seed variations).\n\n## 2. LoRA Hyperparameters\n\n| Parameter | Value |\n|-----------|-------|\n| LoRA rank (r) | 16 |\n| LoRA alpha | 32 |\n| LoRA dropout | 0.05 |\n| Target modules | q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, o_proj, gate_proj, up_proj, down_proj |\n| Learning rate | 2e-4 |\n| LR scheduler | cosine |\n| Warmup ratio | 0.03 |\n| Epochs | 3 |\n| Per-device batch size | 4 |\n| Gradient accumulation | 1 |\n| Max sequence length | 2,048 tokens |\n| Gradient checkpointing | Yes (for VOTA/exception_only) |\n| Optimizer | AdamW (8-bit via bitsandbytes) |\n| Weight decay | 0.01 |\n| bf16 | Yes |\n\n*Note: Verify exact values against `train/train_sft.py` defaults and command-line overrides.*\n\n## 3. Dataset Split Details\n\n### Main dataset (HumanEval + MBPP-sanitized)\n- **Problems**: 591 unique (164 HumanEval + 427 MBPP-sanitized)\n- **Candidate solutions**: Generated by Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct (10 per problem)\n- **Total samples**: 5,910 (591 × 10)\n- **Train/test split**: 80/20 → 4,720 train / 1,190 test\n- **Class distribution**: ~71.51% pass / ~28.49% fail (majority baseline = 71.51%)\n- **Deduplication**: Applied to training set (exact-match on normalized code)\n\n### DebugBench dataset\n- **Source**: DebugBench (Tian et al., 2024) — synthetically injected bugs in LeetCode solutions\n- **Bug types**: logic errors, reference errors, syntax errors\n- **Train/test split**: 1,622 train / 426 test\n- **Class distribution**: ~51.41% pass / ~48.59% fail (near balanced)\n\n## 4. Random Seed Strategy\n\n- **Primary seed**: 42 (all conditions)\n- **Robustness seeds**: 123, 456 (selected conditions)\n- **Seed-tested conditions**:\n  - exception_only (Qwen): seeds 42/123/456 → 99.83% ± 0.00 (perfectly robust, 2/1190 identical errors across all seeds)\n  - exception_only (DeepSeek): seeds 42/123/456 → 99.33%/91.09%/99.33% (seed 123 outlier, LoRA initialization sensitivity)\n  - VOTA (Qwen): seeds 42/456 → 99.41%/99.08%\n  - ae_only (Qwen): seed 42 → 99.92%; seeds 123/456 in progress\n  - label_only: seed 42 only (seeds 123/456 planned)\n- **Other conditions**: single seed (42)\n\n## 5. Evaluation Metrics Definitions\n\n| Metric | Definition |\n|--------|-----------|\n| **Accuracy** | Fraction of correctly classified samples (pass/fail) |\n| **Pass/Fail Precision** | TP/(TP+FP) for each class |\n| **Pass/Fail Recall** | TP/(TP+FN) for each class |\n| **Pass/Fail F1** | Harmonic mean of precision and recall per class |\n| **95% CI** | Wilson score interval for accuracy |\n| **ECE** | Expected Calibration Error — weighted average of |accuracy − confidence| across 10 equal-width bins |\n| **MCE** | Maximum Calibration Error — max |accuracy − confidence| across bins |\n| **Brier Score** | Mean squared error between predicted probability and true label |\n| **McNemar's Test** | Paired test for significance of accuracy difference between two models on same test set (with continuity correction) |\n| **Paired Bootstrap** | 10,000 bootstrap resamples, percentile CI, paired on sample IDs |\n\n## 6. Code & Data Availability Statement\n\n- **Code**: Training, evaluation, and data processing scripts available at [repository URL TBD]\n- **Models**: LoRA adapters for all conditions available at [HuggingFace URL TBD]\n- **Data**: Execution traces generated from HumanEval and MBPP-sanitized using Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct\n  - Raw trace collection via Python `sys.settrace` instrumentation\n  - VOTA compression via deterministic algorithm (no LLM in loop)\n  - DebugBench traces from DebugBench dataset's provided solutions\n- **Base models**: Publicly available\n  - Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct (HuggingFace)\n  - deepseek-ai/deepseek-coder-6.7b-instruct (HuggingFace)\n- **Compute**: All experiments reproducible on a single machine with 2× consumer/prosumer GPUs (~48GB total VRAM)\n- **Licensing**: [TBD — check HumanEval, MBPP, DebugBench licenses]\n",

Training Hardware & Runtime\n\n**Server**: westd-11235 (2× NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition, ~48GB each, ~96GB total)\n**GPU allocation**: cuda:0 + cuda:1 (training uses 1 GPU per condition)\n\n### Per-condition training time (Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct, 3 epochs, seed=42)\n\n| Condition | Wall Clock (s) | GPU Hours | Notes |\n|-----------|---------------|-----------|-------|\n| no_trace | ~1,275 | 0.35 | shortest (no trace tokens) |\n| exception_only | 4,541–7,507 | 1.3–2.1 | varies by seed |\n| ae_only | ~6,398 | ~1.8 | similar to exception_only |\n| loop_only | 7,426 | 2.06 | |\n| vota_no_ae | 6,398 | 1.78 | |\n| VOTA (full) | 8,443 | 2.35 | longest (most trace tokens) |\n| full_trace | 7,852 | 2.18 | |\n| label_only (full_trace + loss mask) | ~7,800 | ~2.2 | similar to full_trace |\n\n### DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct (3 epochs, seed=42)\n\n| Condition | Wall Clock (s) | GPU Hours |\n|-----------|---------------|-----------|\n| no_trace | ~1,200 | ~0.33 |\n| exception_only | 6,246–6,384 | 1.7–1.8 |\n| VOTA | 8,840 | 2.46 |\n| full_trace (v3) | 8,891 | ~2.5 |\n\n### DebugBench (Qwen, 3 epochs, seed=42)\n\n| Condition | Wall Clock (s) | GPU Hours |\n|-----------|---------------|-----------|\n| no_trace | ~800 | ~0.22 |\n| exception_only | 837 | 0.23 |\n| VOTA | ~3,200 | ~0.9 |\n\n**Total GPU compute**: ~50 GPU-hours across all experiments (including failed/killed runs and seed variations).\n\n## 2. LoRA Hyperparameters\n\n| Parameter | Value |\n|-----------|-------|\n| LoRA rank (r) | 16 |\n| LoRA alpha | 32 |\n| LoRA dropout | 0.05 |\n| Target modules | q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, o_proj, gate_proj, up_proj, down_proj |\n| Learning rate | 2e-4 |\n| LR scheduler | linear |\n| Warmup ratio | 0.1 |\n| Epochs | 3 |\n| Per-device batch size | 4 |\n| Gradient accumulation | 8 (effective batch size = 32) |\n| Max sequence length | 2,048 tokens |\n| Gradient checkpointing | Yes (for VOTA/exception_only) |\n| Optimizer | AdamW (fused, via torch) |\n| Weight decay | 0.01 |\n| bf16 | Yes |\n\n*Note: Verify exact values against `train/train_sft.py` defaults and command-line overrides.*\n\n## 3. Dataset Split Details\n\n### Main dataset (HumanEval + MBPP-sanitized)\n- **Problems**: 591 unique (164 HumanEval + 427 MBPP-sanitized)\n- **Candidate solutions**: Generated by Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct (10 per problem)\n- **Total samples**: 5,910 (591 × 10)\n- **Train/test split**: 80/20 → 4,720 train / 1,190 test\n- **Class distribution**: ~71.51% pass / ~28.49% fail (majority baseline = 71.51%)\n- **Deduplication**: Applied to training set (exact-match on normalized code)\n\n### DebugBench dataset\n- **Source**: DebugBench (Tian et al., 2024) — synthetically injected bugs in LeetCode solutions\n- **Bug types**: logic errors, reference errors, syntax errors\n- **Train/test split**: 1,622 train / 426 test\n- **Class distribution**: ~51.41% pass / ~48.59% fail (near balanced)\n\n## 4. Random Seed Strategy\n\n- **Primary seed**: 42 (all conditions)\n- **Robustness seeds**: 123, 456 (selected conditions)\n- **Seed-tested conditions**:\n  - exception_only (Qwen): seeds 42/123/456 → 99.83% ± 0.00 (perfectly robust, 2/1190 identical errors across all seeds)\n  - exception_only (DeepSeek): seeds 42/123/456 → 99.33%/91.09%/99.33% (seed 123 outlier, LoRA initialization sensitivity)\n  - VOTA (Qwen): seeds 42/456 → 99.41%/99.08%\n  - ae_only (Qwen): seed 42 → 99.92%; seeds 123/456 in progress\n  - label_only: seed 42 only (seeds 123/456 planned)\n- **Other conditions**: single seed (42)\n\n## 5. Evaluation Metrics Definitions\n\n| Metric | Definition |\n|--------|-----------|\n| **Accuracy** | Fraction of correctly classified samples (pass/fail) |\n| **Pass/Fail Precision** | TP/(TP+FP) for each class |\n| **Pass/Fail Recall** | TP/(TP+FN) for each class |\n| **Pass/Fail F1** | Harmonic mean of precision and recall per class |\n| **95% CI** | Wilson score interval for accuracy |\n| **ECE** | Expected Calibration Error — weighted average of |accuracy − confidence| across 10 equal-width bins |\n| **MCE** | Maximum Calibration Error — max |accuracy − confidence| across bins |\n| **Brier Score** | Mean squared error between predicted probability and true label |\n| **McNemar's Test** | Paired test for significance of accuracy difference between two models on same test set (with continuity correction) |\n| **Paired Bootstrap** | 10,000 bootstrap resamples, percentile CI, paired on sample IDs |\n\n## 6. Code & Data Availability Statement\n\n- **Code**: Training, evaluation, and data processing scripts available at [repository URL TBD]\n- **Models**: LoRA adapters for all conditions available at [HuggingFace URL TBD]\n- **Data**: Execution traces generated from HumanEval and MBPP-sanitized using Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct\n  - Raw trace collection via Python `sys.settrace` instrumentation\n  - VOTA compression via deterministic algorithm (no LLM in loop)\n  - DebugBench traces from DebugBench dataset's provided solutions\n- **Base models**: Publicly available\n  - Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct (HuggingFace)\n  - deepseek-ai/deepseek-coder-6.7b-instruct (HuggingFace)\n- **Compute**: All experiments reproducible on a single machine with 2× consumer/prosumer GPUs (~48GB total VRAM)\n- **Licensing**: [TBD — check HumanEval, MBPP, DebugBench licenses]\n",
